  /**
   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * Summary: Applies the configured compression mode to a byte array.
   * Algorithm:
   *   if compression == zlib:
   *       return zlibCompress(data)
   *   else:
   *       return copy of data
   * Related Features: compression, compressionMethod
   * Related Classes: CompressionMethod
   * Input: data : EByteArray - the bytes to process.
   * Output: EByteArray - compressed bytes when zlib mode is active, otherwise unchanged bytes.
   * Pre-condition: compression mode is "none" or "zlib"; data is not null.
   * Post-condition: The returned bytes can be reversed by unprocessData with the same compression mode.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public byte[] processData(final byte[] data)
  {
    if ("zlib".equals(getCompression()))
    {
      return zlibCompress(data);
    }
    else
    {
      return data.clone();
    }
  }

  /**
   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * Summary: Reverses the effect of processData, typically decompressing a zlib byte stream.
   * Algorithm:
   *   if compression == zlib:
   *       return zlibDecompress(data)
   *   else:
   *       return copy of data
   * Related Features: compression, compressionMethod
   * Related Classes: CompressionMethod
   * Input: data : EByteArray - processed bytes.
   * Output: EByteArray - original bytes before processData was applied.
   * Pre-condition: data was produced by processData with the same compression mode.
   * Post-condition: Decompresses zlib data when configured; otherwise returns unchanged data.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public byte[] unprocessData(final byte[] data)
  {
    if ("zlib".equals(getCompression()))
    {
      return zlibDecompress(data);
    }
    else
    {
      return data.clone();
    }
  }

  /**
   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * Summary: Computes an optional SHA-256 integrity digest for a byte array.
   * Algorithm:
   *   if verification mode is "sha256":
   *       digest = SHA-256(data)
   *       return lowercaseHex(digest) left-padded with zeros to 64 characters
   *   else:
   *       return ""
   * Related Features: verification, verificationMethod
   * Related Classes: VerificationMethod
   * Input: data : EByteArray - the bytes to hash.
   * Output: String - a lowercase hexadecimal SHA-256 digest of length 64 when mode is "sha256", otherwise an empty string.
   * Pre-condition: data is not null; verification mode is either "none" or "sha256".
   * Post-condition: No data is modified; the returned digest deterministically represents the input.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public String calculateHash(final byte[] data)
  {
    if ("sha256".equals(getVerification()))
    {
      try
      {
        MessageDigest digest = MessageDigest.getInstance("SHA-256");
        byte[] hash = digest.digest(data);
        StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
        for (byte b : hash)
        {
          sb.append(String.format("%02x", b));
        }
        String hex = sb.toString();
        while (hex.length() < 64)
        {
          hex = "0" + hex;
        }
        return hex;
      }
      catch (NoSuchAlgorithmException e)
      {
        throw new RuntimeException(e);
      }
    }
    else
    {
      return "";
    }
  }

  /**
   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * Summary: Prepares binary data for emoji encoding by base64-encoding the content and preserving MIME type and size.
   * Algorithm:
   *   content = Base64.encode(data)
   *   return new PreparedData(content, mimeType, data.length)
   * Related Features: None
   * Related Classes: PreparedData
   * Input: mimeType : String, data : EByteArray
   * Output: PreparedData - contains Base64 content, MIME type, and original byte size.
   * Pre-condition: mimeType and data are not null.
   * Post-condition: preparedData.size == data.length; preparedData.content is valid Base64 text.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public PreparedData prepareBinaryData(final String mimeType, final byte[] data)
  {
    String content = Base64.getEncoder().encodeToString(data);
    PreparedData prepared = FreddyrodgerFactory.eINSTANCE.createPreparedData();
    prepared.setContent(content);
    prepared.setMimeType(mimeType);
    prepared.setSize(data.length);
    return prepared;
  }

  /**
   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * Summary: Restores original binary bytes from a PreparedData object.
   * Algorithm:
   *   bytes = Base64.decode(preparedData.content)
   *   if bytes.length != preparedData.size:
   *       throw IllegalArgumentException("size mismatch")
   *   return bytes
   * Related Features: None
   * Related Classes: PreparedData
   * Input: preparedData : PreparedData - contains Base64 content and original size.
   * Output: EByteArray - the decoded original binary content.
   * Pre-condition: preparedData.content is valid Base64; preparedData.size equals the decoded length.
   * Post-condition: The returned byte array has the same length as preparedData.size.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public byte[] restoreBinaryData(final PreparedData preparedData)
  {
    byte[] bytes = Base64.getDecoder().decode(preparedData.getContent());
    if (bytes.length != preparedData.getSize())
    {
      throw new IllegalArgumentException("size mismatch");
    }
    return bytes;
  }

  /**
   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * Summary: Restores binary bytes from a map containing the JSON fields content, mime_type, and size.
   * Algorithm:
   *   content = (String) data.get("content")
   *   mime = (String) data.get("mime_type")
   *   size = ((Number) data.get("size")).intValue()
   *   return restoreBinaryData(new PreparedData(content, mime, size))
   * Related Features: restoreBinaryData
   * Related Classes: PreparedData
   * Input: data : EMap - map with content, mime_type, and size entries.
   * Output: EByteArray - the original binary content.
   * Pre-condition: data contains the required fields; content is valid Base64; size matches decoded length.
   * Post-condition: Returns the original binary data without modifying the input map.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public byte[] restoreBinaryDataFromMap(final Map<String, Object> data)
  {
    String content = (String) data.get("content");
    String mime = (String) data.get("mime_type");
    int size = ((Number) data.get("size")).intValue();

    PreparedData prepared = FreddyrodgerFactory.eINSTANCE.createPreparedData();
    prepared.setContent(content);
    prepared.setMimeType(mime);
    prepared.setSize(size);
    return restoreBinaryData(prepared);
  }

  /**
   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * Summary: Suggests a recipe based on file size.
   * Algorithm:
   *   if size < 1024: return "quick"
   *   if size < 10240: return "light"
   *   if size < 102400: return "classic"
   *   return "gourmet"
   * Related Features: None
   * Related Classes: None
   * Input: size : int - file size in bytes.
   * Output: String - one of "quick", "light", "classic", or "gourmet".
   * Pre-condition: size is non-negative.
   * Post-condition: The returned recipe is the suggested recipe for the given size.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public String suggestRecipe(final int size)
  {
    if (size < 1024)
    {
      return "quick";
    }
    if (size < 10240)
    {
      return "light";
    }
    if (size < 102400)
    {
      return "classic";
    }
    return "gourmet";
  }

  // ----- private helper methods -----

  private int countEmojiSymbols(final String encoded)
  {
    if (encoded == null || encoded.isEmpty())
    {
      return 0;
    }
    Map<String, Integer> rev = getReverseMap();
    if (rev == null || rev.isEmpty())
    {
      return 0;
    }
    int count = 0;
    int i = 0;
    while (i < encoded.length())
    {
      String symbol = null;
      for (String key : rev.keySet())
      {
        if (key != null && key.length() > 0 && encoded.startsWith(key, i))
        {
          if (symbol == null || key.length() > symbol.length())
          {
            symbol = key;
          }
        }
      }
      if (symbol == null)
      {
        throw new IllegalArgumentException("unknown symbol");
      }
      count++;
      i += symbol.length();
    }
    return count;
  }

  private byte[] zlibCompress(final byte[] data)
  {
    Deflater deflater = new Deflater();
    deflater.setInput(data);
    deflater.finish();
    ByteArrayOutputStream baos = new ByteArrayOutputStream();
    byte[] buffer = new byte[8192];
    while (!deflater.finished())
    {
      int len = deflater.deflate(buffer);
      baos.write(buffer, 0, len);
    }
    deflater.end();
    return baos.toByteArray();
  }

  private byte[] zlibDecompress(final byte[] data)
  {
    Inflater inflater = new Inflater();
    inflater.setInput(data);
    ByteArrayOutputStream baos = new ByteArrayOutputStream();
    byte[] buffer = new byte[8192];
    try
    {
      while (!inflater.finished())
      {
        int len = inflater.inflate(buffer);
        if (len == 0 && inflater.needsInput())
        {
          break;
        }
        baos.write(buffer, 0, len);
      }
    }
    catch (DataFormatException e)
    {
      throw new RuntimeException(e);
    }
    finally
    {
      inflater.end();
    }
    return baos.toByteArray();
  }

  private String toJson(final Map<String, Object> map)
  {
    StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
    sb.append('{');
    boolean first = true;
    for (Map.Entry<String, Object> entry : map.entrySet())
    {
      if (!first)
      {
        sb.append(',');
      }
      first = false;
      sb.append('"').append(escapeJson(entry.getKey())).append("\":");
      appendJsonValue(sb, entry.getValue());
    }
    sb.append('}');
    return sb.toString();
  }

  private String toJsonArray(final Iterable<?> iterable)
  {
    StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
    sb.append('[');
    boolean first = true;
    for (Object value : iterable)
    {
      if (!first)
      {
        sb.append(',');
      }
      first = false;
      appendJsonValue(sb, value);
    }
    sb.append(']');
    return sb.toString();
  }

  @SuppressWarnings("unchecked")
  private void appendJsonValue(final StringBuilder sb, final Object value)
  {
    if (value == null)
    {
      sb.append("null");
    }
    else if (value instanceof Number)
    {
      sb.append(value.toString());
    }
    else if (value instanceof Boolean)
    {
      sb.append(value.toString());
    }
    else if (value instanceof Map)
    {
      sb.append(toJson((Map<String, Object>) value));
    }
    else if (value instanceof Iterable)
    {
      sb.append(toJsonArray((Iterable<?>) value));
    }
    else
    {
      sb.append('"').append(escapeJson(String.valueOf(value))).append('"');
    }
  }

  private String escapeJson(final String s)
  {
    StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
    for (char c : s.toCharArray())
    {
      switch (c)
      {
        case '"': sb.append("\\\""); break;
        case '\\': sb.append("\\\\"); break;
        case '\n': sb.append("\\n"); break;
        case '\r': sb.append("\\r"); break;
        case '\t': sb.append("\\t"); break;
        case '\b': sb.append("\\b"); break;
        case '\f': sb.append("\\f"); break;
        default:
          if (c < 0x20)
          {
            sb.append(String.format("\\u%04x", (int) c));
          }
          else
          {
            sb.append(c);
          }
      }
    }
    return sb.toString();
  }

  private Map<String, Object> parseJson(final String json)
  {
    int[] pos = { 0 };
    Object value = parseJsonValue(json, pos);
    if (!(value instanceof Map))
    {
      throw new IllegalArgumentException("Invalid JSON");
    }
    @SuppressWarnings("unchecked")
    Map<String, Object> map = (Map<String, Object>) value;
    return map;
  }

  private Object parseJsonValue(final String s, final int[] pos)
  {
    skipJsonWhitespace(s, pos);
    char c = s.charAt(pos[0]);
    switch (c)
    {
      case '{': return parseJsonObject(s, pos);
      case '[': return parseJsonArray(s, pos);
      case '"': return parseJsonString(s, pos);
      case 't': pos[0] += 4; return Boolean.TRUE;
      case 'f': pos[0] += 5; return Boolean.FALSE;
      case 'n': pos[0] += 4; return null;
      default: return parseJsonNumber(s, pos);
    }
  }

  @SuppressWarnings("unchecked")
  private Map<String, Object> parseJsonObject(final String s, final int[] pos)
  {
    Map<String, Object> map = new HashMap<String, Object>();
    pos[0]++;
    skipJsonWhitespace(s, pos);
    if (s.charAt(pos[0]) == '}')
    {
      pos[0]++;
      return map;
    }
    while (true)
    {
      skipJsonWhitespace(s, pos);
      String key = parseJsonString(s, pos);
      skipJsonWhitespace(s, pos);
      if (s.charAt(pos[0]) != ':')
      {
        throw new IllegalArgumentException("Expected ':'");
      }
      pos[0]++;
      Object value = parseJsonValue(s, pos);
      map.put(key, value);
      skipJsonWhitespace(s, pos);
      char c = s.charAt(pos[0]);
      if (c == ',')
      {
        pos[0]++;
        continue;
      }
      if (c == '}')
      {
        pos[0]++;
        break;
      }
      throw new IllegalArgumentException("Expected ',' or '}'");
    }
    return map;
  }

  @SuppressWarnings("unchecked")
  private Object parseJsonArray(final String s, final int[] pos)
  {
    org.eclipse.emf.common.util.BasicEList<Object> list =
      new org.eclipse.emf.common.util.BasicEList<Object>();
    pos[0]++;
    skipJsonWhitespace(s, pos);
    if (s.charAt(pos[0]) == ']')
    {
      pos[0]++;
      return list;
    }
    while (true)
    {
      skipJsonWhitespace(s, pos);
      Object value = parseJsonValue(s, pos);
      list.add(value);
      skipJsonWhitespace(s, pos);
      char c = s.charAt(pos[0]);
      if (c == ',')
      {
        pos[0]++;
        continue;
      }
      if (c == ']')
      {
        pos[0]++;
        break;
      }
      throw new IllegalArgumentException("Expected ',' or ']'");
    }
    return list;
  }

  private String parseJsonString(final String s, final int[] pos)
  {
    pos[0]++;
    StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
    while (true)
    {
      char c = s.charAt(pos[0]++);
      if (c == '"')
      {
        break;
      }
      if (c == '\\')
      {
        char esc = s.charAt(pos[0]++);
        switch (esc)
        {
          case '"': sb.append('"'); break;
          case '\\': sb.append('\\'); break;
          case '/': sb.append('/'); break;
          case 'n': sb.append('\n'); break;
          case 'r': sb.append('\r'); break;
          case 't': sb.append('\t'); break;
          case 'b': sb.append('\b'); break;
          case 'f': sb.append('\f'); break;
          case 'u':
            String hex = s.substring(pos[0], pos[0] + 4);
            pos[0] += 4;
            sb.append((char) Integer.parseInt(hex, 16));
            break;
          default:
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("Invalid escape sequence");
        }
      }
      else
      {
        sb.append(c);
      }
    }
    return sb.toString();
  }

  private Object parseJsonNumber(final String s, final int[] pos)
  {
    int start = pos[0];
    while (pos[0] < s.length())
    {
      char c = s.charAt(pos[0]);
      if (c == ',' || c == '}' || c == ']' || Character.isWhitespace(c))
      {
        break;
      }
      pos[0]++;
    }
    String num = s.substring(start, pos[0]);
    try
    {
      return Integer.valueOf(num);
    }
    catch (NumberFormatException e)
    {
      return Double.valueOf(num);
    }
  }

  private void skipJsonWhitespace(final String s, final int[] pos)
  {
    while (pos[0] < s.length() && Character.isWhitespace(s.charAt(pos[0])))
    {
      pos[0]++;
    }
  }

  public EList<Map<String, Object>> batchProcess(final String filePattern, final String outputDir, final String operation)
  {
      try {
            Path outDir = Path.of(outputDir);
            Files.createDirectories(outDir);
    
            Path patternPath = Path.of(filePattern);
            Path directory = patternPath.getParent();
    
            if (directory == null) {
                directory = Path.of(".");
            }
    
            String glob = patternPath.getFileName().toString();
    
            PathMatcher matcher = directory
                .getFileSystem()
                .getPathMatcher("glob:" + glob);
    
            BasicEList<Path> files = new BasicEList<Path>();
    
            try (DirectoryStream<Path> stream =
                    Files.newDirectoryStream(directory)) {
    
                for (Path entry : stream) {
                    if (Files.isRegularFile(entry)
                            && matcher.matches(entry.getFileName())) {
                        files.add(entry);
                    }
                }
            }
    
            BasicEList<Map<String, Object>> results =
                new BasicEList<Map<String, Object>>();
    
            for (Path inputFile : files) {
                String stem = inputFile.getFileName().toString();
                int dotIndex = stem.lastIndexOf('.');
    
                if (dotIndex > 0) {
                    stem = stem.substring(0, dotIndex);
                }
    
                Path outputPath;
    
                if ("encode".equals(operation)) {
                    outputPath = outDir.resolve(stem + ".emoji");
                } else {
                    String mimeType =
                        URLConnection.guessContentTypeFromName(
                            inputFile.getFileName().toString()
                        );
    
                    String extension =
                        mimeType != null ? ".bin" : ".bin";
    
                    outputPath = outDir.resolve(
                        "decoded_" + stem + extension
                    );
                }
    
                try {
                    Map<String, Object> statistics = processFile(
                        inputFile.toString(),
                        outputPath.toString(),
                        operation
                    );
    
                    statistics.put("file", inputFile.toString());
                    statistics.put("success", Boolean.TRUE);
    
                    results.add(statistics);
                } catch (Exception exception) {
                    Map<String, Object> errorResult =
                        new LinkedHashMap<String, Object>();
    
                    errorResult.put("file", inputFile.toString());
                    errorResult.put("success", Boolean.FALSE);
                    errorResult.put("error", exception.getMessage());
    
                    results.add(errorResult);
                }
            }
    
            return results;
        } catch (IOException exception) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(
                "Batch processing error: " + exception.getMessage(),
                exception
            );
        }
  }

   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * @model annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='manual'"
   * @generated

  public EList<Map<String, Object>> batchProcess(final String filePattern, final String outputDir, final String operation)
  {
      try {
            Path outDir = Path.of(outputDir);
            Files.createDirectories(outDir);
    
            Path patternPath = Path.of(filePattern);
            Path directory = patternPath.getParent();
    
            if (directory == null) {
                directory = Path.of(".");
            }
    
            String glob = patternPath.getFileName().toString();
    
            PathMatcher matcher = directory
                .getFileSystem()
                .getPathMatcher("glob:" + glob);
    
            BasicEList<Path> files = new BasicEList<Path>();
    
            try (DirectoryStream<Path> stream =
                    Files.newDirectoryStream(directory)) {
    
                for (Path entry : stream) {
                    if (Files.isRegularFile(entry)
                            && matcher.matches(entry.getFileName())) {
                        files.add(entry);
                    }
                }
            }
    
            BasicEList<Map<String, Object>> results =
                new BasicEList<Map<String, Object>>();
    
            for (Path inputFile : files) {
                String stem = inputFile.getFileName().toString();
                int dotIndex = stem.lastIndexOf('.');
    
                if (dotIndex > 0) {
                    stem = stem.substring(0, dotIndex);
                }
    
                Path outputPath;
    
                if ("encode".equals(operation)) {
                    outputPath = outDir.resolve(stem + ".emoji");
                } else {
                    String mimeType =
                        URLConnection.guessContentTypeFromName(
                            inputFile.getFileName().toString()
                        );
    
                    String extension =
                        mimeType != null ? ".bin" : ".bin";
    
                    outputPath = outDir.resolve(
                        "decoded_" + stem + extension
                    );
                }
    
                try {
                    Map<String, Object> statistics = processFile(
                        inputFile.toString(),
                        outputPath.toString(),
                        operation
                    );
    
                    statistics.put("file", inputFile.toString());
                    statistics.put("success", Boolean.TRUE);
    
                    results.add(statistics);
                } catch (Exception exception) {
                    Map<String, Object> errorResult =
                        new LinkedHashMap<String, Object>();
    
                    errorResult.put("file", inputFile.toString());
                    errorResult.put("success", Boolean.FALSE);
                    errorResult.put("error", exception.getMessage());
    
                    results.add(errorResult);
                }
            }
    
            return results;
        } catch (IOException exception) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(
                "Batch processing error: " + exception.getMessage(),
                exception
            );
        }
  }

   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->

   * @model annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='manual'"
   * @generated
   */
  public EList<Map<String, Object>> batchProcess(final String filePattern, final String outputDir, final String operation)
  {
      try {
            Path outDir = Path.of(outputDir);
            Files.createDirectories(outDir);
    
            Path patternPath = Path.of(filePattern);
            Path directory = patternPath.getParent();
    
            if (directory == null) {
                directory = Path.of(".");
            }
    
            String glob = patternPath.getFileName().toString();
    
            PathMatcher matcher = directory
                .getFileSystem()
                .getPathMatcher("glob:" + glob);
    
            BasicEList<Path> files = new BasicEList<Path>();
    
            try (DirectoryStream<Path> stream =
                    Files.newDirectoryStream(directory)) {
    
                for (Path entry : stream) {
                    if (Files.isRegularFile(entry)
                            && matcher.matches(entry.getFileName())) {
                        files.add(entry);
                    }
                }
            }
    
            BasicEList<Map<String, Object>> results =
                new BasicEList<Map<String, Object>>();
    
            for (Path inputFile : files) {
                String stem = inputFile.getFileName().toString();
                int dotIndex = stem.lastIndexOf('.');
    
                if (dotIndex > 0) {
                    stem = stem.substring(0, dotIndex);
                }
    
                Path outputPath;
    
                if ("encode".equals(operation)) {
                    outputPath = outDir.resolve(stem + ".emoji");
                } else {
                    String mimeType =
                        URLConnection.guessContentTypeFromName(
                            inputFile.getFileName().toString()
                        );
    
                    String extension =
                        mimeType != null ? ".bin" : ".bin";
    
                    outputPath = outDir.resolve(
                        "decoded_" + stem + extension
                    );
                }
    
                try {
                    Map<String, Object> statistics = processFile(
                        inputFile.toString(),
                        outputPath.toString(),
                        operation
                    );
    
                    statistics.put("file", inputFile.toString());
                    statistics.put("success", Boolean.TRUE);
    
                    results.add(statistics);
                } catch (Exception exception) {
                    Map<String, Object> errorResult =
                        new LinkedHashMap<String, Object>();
    
                    errorResult.put("file", inputFile.toString());
                    errorResult.put("success", Boolean.FALSE);
                    errorResult.put("error", exception.getMessage());
    
                    results.add(errorResult);
                }
            }
    
            return results;
        } catch (IOException exception) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(
                "Batch processing error: " + exception.getMessage(),
                exception
            );
        }
  }

You will be given a **Java Class** that is originally generated by EMF code generator with `suppressInterfaces=true` and later is completed by LLMs. Your task is to fix the **Compilation Errors** by taking additional contextual information into account.

Complete this task by following the guidelines below.
1. Compute context information
  1.1 Consider the related classes in the Javadocs.
  1.2 Locate these files in your project.
  1.3 Generate the class summarization.
2. Read the error messages carefully.
3. Do your best to fix the errors locally without introducing new types, fields, and methods.
4. You should not remove faulty methods to eliminate compilation errors.
5. Do not change parts without errors.

## EMF Coding Style
- When possible, call getter/setter methods to access field values. Do not read/write raw fields derived from the class diagram.
- The getter of a boolean attribute `bv` looks like `isBv()`, e.g., `isIsTrue()` for `isTrue`.
- When possibe, use the creation factory for object creation.
- To create an EList instance, import and use `org.eclipse.emf.common.util.BasicEList`.

Complete this task by following the guidelines below.
1. Compute context information
  1.1 Consider the related classes in the Javadocs.
  1.2 Locate these files in your project.
  1.3 Generate the class summarization.
2. Read the error messages carefully.
3. Do your best to fix the errors locally without introducing new types, fields, and methods.
4. You should not remove faulty methods to eliminate compilation errors.
5. Do not change parts without errors.

## EMF Coding Style
- When possible, call getter/setter methods to access field values. Do not read/write raw fields derived from the class diagram.
- The getter of a boolean attribute `bv` looks like `isBv()`, e.g., `isIsTrue()` for `isTrue`.
- When possibe, use the creation factory for object creation.
- To create an EList instance, import and use `org.eclipse.emf.common.util.BasicEList`.

  public EList<Map<String, Object>> batchProcess(final String filePattern, final String outputDir, final String operation)
  {
      try {
            Path outDir = Path.of(outputDir);
            Files.createDirectories(outDir);
    
            Path patternPath = Path.of(filePattern);
            Path directory = patternPath.getParent();
    
            if (directory == null) {
                directory = Path.of(".");
            }
    
            String glob = patternPath.getFileName().toString();
    
            PathMatcher matcher = directory
                .getFileSystem()
                .getPathMatcher("glob:" + glob);
    
            BasicEList<Path> files = new BasicEList<Path>();
    
            try (DirectoryStream<Path> stream =
                    Files.newDirectoryStream(directory)) {
    
                for (Path entry : stream) {
                    if (Files.isRegularFile(entry)
                            && matcher.matches(entry.getFileName())) {
                        files.add(entry);
                    }
                }
            }
    
            BasicEList<Map<String, Object>> results =
                new BasicEList<Map<String, Object>>();
    
            for (Path inputFile : files) {
                String stem = inputFile.getFileName().toString();
                int dotIndex = stem.lastIndexOf('.');
    
                if (dotIndex > 0) {
                    stem = stem.substring(0, dotIndex);
                }
    
                Path outputPath;
    
                if ("encode".equals(operation)) {
                    outputPath = outDir.resolve(stem + ".emoji");
                } else {
                    String mimeType =
                        URLConnection.guessContentTypeFromName(
                            inputFile.getFileName().toString()
                        );
    
                    String extension =
                        mimeType != null ? ".bin" : ".bin";
    
                    outputPath = outDir.resolve(
                        "decoded_" + stem + extension
                    );
                }
    
                try {
                    Map<String, Object> statistics = processFile(
                        inputFile.toString(),
                        outputPath.toString(),
                        operation
                    );
    
                    statistics.put("file", inputFile.toString());
                    statistics.put("success", Boolean.TRUE);
    
                    results.add(statistics);
                } catch (Exception exception) {
                    Map<String, Object> errorResult =
                        new LinkedHashMap<String, Object>();
    
                    errorResult.put("file", inputFile.toString());
                    errorResult.put("success", Boolean.FALSE);
                    errorResult.put("error", exception.getMessage());
    
                    results.add(errorResult);
                }
            }
    
            return results;
        } catch (IOException exception) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(
                "Batch processing error: " + exception.getMessage(),
                exception
            );
        }
  }

Complete this task by following the guidelines below.

  1.2 Locate these files in your project.

2. Read the error messages carefully.

5. Do not change parts without errors.

## EMF Coding Style
- When possible, call getter/setter methods to access field values. Do not read/write raw fields derived from the class diagram.
- The getter of a boolean attribute `bv` looks like `isBv()`, e.g., `isIsTrue()` for `isTrue`.
- When possibe, use the creation factory for object creation.
- To create an EList instance, import and use `org.eclipse.emf.common.util.BasicEList`.

  public EList<Map<String, Object>> batchProcess(final String filePattern, final String outputDir, final String operation)
  {
      try {
            Path outDir = Path.of(outputDir);
            Files.createDirectories(outDir);
    
            Path patternPath = Path.of(filePattern);
            Path directory = patternPath.getParent();
    
            if (directory == null) {
                directory = Path.of(".");
            }
    
            String glob = patternPath.getFileName().toString();
    
            PathMatcher matcher = directory
                .getFileSystem()
                .getPathMatcher("glob:" + glob);
    
            BasicEList<Path> files = new BasicEList<Path>();
    
            try (DirectoryStream<Path> stream =
                    Files.newDirectoryStream(directory)) {
    
                for (Path entry : stream) {
                    if (Files.isRegularFile(entry)
                            && matcher.matches(entry.getFileName())) {
                        files.add(entry);
                    }
                }
            }
    
            BasicEList<Map<String, Object>> results =
                new BasicEList<Map<String, Object>>();
    
            for (Path inputFile : files) {
                String stem = inputFile.getFileName().toString();
                int dotIndex = stem.lastIndexOf('.');
    
                if (dotIndex > 0) {
                    stem = stem.substring(0, dotIndex);
                }
    
                Path outputPath;
    
                if ("encode".equals(operation)) {
                    outputPath = outDir.resolve(stem + ".emoji");
                } else {
                    String mimeType =
                        URLConnection.guessContentTypeFromName(
                            inputFile.getFileName().toString()
                        );
    
                    String extension =
                        mimeType != null ? ".bin" : ".bin";
    
                    outputPath = outDir.resolve(
                        "decoded_" + stem + extension
                    );
                }
    
                try {
                    Map<String, Object> statistics = processFile(
                        inputFile.toString(),
                        outputPath.toString(),
                        operation
                    );
    
                    statistics.put("file", inputFile.toString());
                    statistics.put("success", Boolean.TRUE);
    
                    results.add(statistics);
                } catch (Exception exception) {
                    Map<String, Object> errorResult =
                        new LinkedHashMap<String, Object>();
    
                    errorResult.put("file", inputFile.toString());
                    errorResult.put("success", Boolean.FALSE);
                    errorResult.put("error", exception.getMessage());
    
                    results.add(errorResult);
                }
            }
    
            return results;
        } catch (IOException exception) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(
                "Batch processing error: " + exception.getMessage(),
                exception
            );
        }
  }

## EMF Coding Style
- When possible, call getter/setter methods to access field values. Do not read/write raw fields derived from the class diagram.
- The getter of a boolean attribute `bv` looks like `isBv()`, e.g., `isIsTrue()` for `isTrue`.
- When possibe, use the creation factory for object creation.
- To create an EList instance, import and use `org.eclipse.emf.common.util.BasicEList`.

  public EList<Map<String, Object>> batchProcess(final String filePattern, final String outputDir, final String operation)
  {
      try {
            Path outDir = Path.of(outputDir);
            Files.createDirectories(outDir);
    
            Path patternPath = Path.of(filePattern);
            Path directory = patternPath.getParent();
    
            if (directory == null) {
                directory = Path.of(".");
            }
    
            String glob = patternPath.getFileName().toString();
    
            PathMatcher matcher = directory
                .getFileSystem()
                .getPathMatcher("glob:" + glob);
    
            BasicEList<Path> files = new BasicEList<Path>();
    
            try (DirectoryStream<Path> stream =
                    Files.newDirectoryStream(directory)) {
    
                for (Path entry : stream) {
                    if (Files.isRegularFile(entry)
                            && matcher.matches(entry.getFileName())) {
                        files.add(entry);
                    }
                }
            }
    
            BasicEList<Map<String, Object>> results =
                new BasicEList<Map<String, Object>>();
    
            for (Path inputFile : files) {
                String stem = inputFile.getFileName().toString();
                int dotIndex = stem.lastIndexOf('.');
    
                if (dotIndex > 0) {
                    stem = stem.substring(0, dotIndex);
                }
    
                Path outputPath;
    
                if ("encode".equals(operation)) {
                    outputPath = outDir.resolve(stem + ".emoji");
                } else {
                    String mimeType =
                        URLConnection.guessContentTypeFromName(
                            inputFile.getFileName().toString()
                        );
    
                    String extension =
                        mimeType != null ? ".bin" : ".bin";
    
                    outputPath = outDir.resolve(
                        "decoded_" + stem + extension
                    );
                }
    
                try {
                    Map<String, Object> statistics = processFile(
                        inputFile.toString(),
                        outputPath.toString(),
                        operation
                    );
    
                    statistics.put("file", inputFile.toString());
                    statistics.put("success", Boolean.TRUE);
    
                    results.add(statistics);
                } catch (Exception exception) {
                    Map<String, Object> errorResult =
                        new LinkedHashMap<String, Object>();
    
                    errorResult.put("file", inputFile.toString());
                    errorResult.put("success", Boolean.FALSE);
                    errorResult.put("error", exception.getMessage());
    
                    results.add(errorResult);
                }
            }
    
            return results;
        } catch (IOException exception) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(
                "Batch processing error: " + exception.getMessage(),
                exception
            );
        }

Complete this task by following the guidelines below.
1. Compute context information
  1.1 Consider the related classes in the Javadocs.
  1.2 Locate these files in your project.
  1.3 Generate the class summarization.
2. Read the error messages carefully.
3. Do your best to fix the errors locally without introducing new types, fields, and methods.
4. You should not remove faulty methods to eliminate compilation errors.
5. Do not change parts without errors.

## EMF Coding Style
- When possible, call getter/setter methods to access field values. Do not read/write raw fields derived from the class diagram.
- The getter of a boolean attribute `bv` looks like `isBv()`, e.g., `isIsTrue()` for `isTrue`.
- When possibe, use the creation factory for object creation.
- To create an EList instance, import and use `org.eclipse.emf.common.util.BasicEList`.

  public EList<Map<String, Object>> batchProcess(final String filePattern, final String outputDir, final String operation)
  {
      try {
            Path outDir = Path.of(outputDir);
            Files.createDirectories(outDir);
    
            Path patternPath = Path.of(filePattern);
            Path directory = patternPath.getParent();
    
            if (directory == null) {
                directory = Path.of(".");
            }
    
            String glob = patternPath.getFileName().toString();
    
            PathMatcher matcher = directory
                .getFileSystem()
                .getPathMatcher("glob:" + glob);
    
            BasicEList<Path> files = new BasicEList<Path>();
    
            try (DirectoryStream<Path> stream =
                    Files.newDirectoryStream(directory)) {
    
                for (Path entry : stream) {
                    if (Files.isRegularFile(entry)
                            && matcher.matches(entry.getFileName())) {
                        files.add(entry);
                    }
                }
            }
    
            BasicEList<Map<String, Object>> results =
                new BasicEList<Map<String, Object>>();
    
            for (Path inputFile : files) {
                String stem = inputFile.getFileName().toString();
                int dotIndex = stem.lastIndexOf('.');
    
                if (dotIndex > 0) {
                    stem = stem.substring(0, dotIndex);
                }
    
                Path outputPath;
    
                if ("encode".equals(operation)) {
                    outputPath = outDir.resolve(stem + ".emoji");
                } else {
                    String mimeType =
                        URLConnection.guessContentTypeFromName(
                            inputFile.getFileName().toString()
                        );
    
                    String extension =
                        mimeType != null ? ".bin" : ".bin";
    
                    outputPath = outDir.resolve(
                        "decoded_" + stem + extension
                    );
                }
    
                try {
                    Map<String, Object> statistics = processFile(
                        inputFile.toString(),
                        outputPath.toString(),
                        operation
                    );
    
                    statistics.put("file", inputFile.toString());
                    statistics.put("success", Boolean.TRUE);
    
                    results.add(statistics);
                } catch (Exception exception) {
                    Map<String, Object> errorResult =
                        new LinkedHashMap<String, Object>();
    
                    errorResult.put("file", inputFile.toString());
                    errorResult.put("success", Boolean.FALSE);
                    errorResult.put("error", exception.getMessage());
    
                    results.add(errorResult);
                }
            }
    
            return results;
        } catch (IOException exception) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(
                "Batch processing error: " + exception.getMessage(),
                exception
            );
        }
  }

  {
      try {

            Files.createDirectories(outDir);
    
            Path patternPath = Path.of(filePattern);
            Path directory = patternPath.getParent();
    
            if (directory == null) {
                directory = Path.of(".");
            }
    
            String glob = patternPath.getFileName().toString();
    
            PathMatcher matcher = directory
                .getFileSystem()
                .getPathMatcher("glob:" + glob);
    
            BasicEList<Path> files = new BasicEList<Path>();
    
            try (DirectoryStream<Path> stream =

            for (Path inputFile : files) {
                String stem = inputFile.getFileName().toString();

                if (dotIndex > 0) {
                    stem = stem.substring(0, dotIndex);
                }
    
                Path outputPath;
    
                if ("encode".equals(operation)) {
                    outputPath = outDir.resolve(stem + ".emoji");
                } else {
                    String mimeType =
                        URLConnection.guessContentTypeFromName(
                            inputFile.getFileName().toString()
                        );
    
                    String extension =
                        mimeType != null ? ".bin" : ".bin";
    
                    outputPath = outDir.resolve(
                        "decoded_" + stem + extension

                }
    
                try {
                    Map<String, Object> statistics = processFile(
                        inputFile.toString(),

                        operation
                    );

                    statistics.put("success", Boolean.TRUE);
    
                    results.add(statistics);
                } catch (Exception exception) {
                    Map<String, Object> errorResult =

                    errorResult.put("success", Boolean.FALSE);

            }
